# Fullseye — ViEW2026

Combiner simulation physique et traitement d'images avec l'IA, et vérifier le résultat par rapport à la vérité terrain.

Touchez une vignette pour ouvrir la vidéo ou la figure (▶ = animé).

## Essayez par vous-même

Manipulez vous-même les figures de l'article, même sur téléphone. Changez de méthode avec les onglets et faites pivoter les vues 3-D en les faisant glisser.

- **[CAO contre nuage de points scanné](../viewers/poc_cad_scan_deviation.html?lang=fr)**: Le recalage absorbe les défauts : une pièce de valeur vraie 1.2 µm montre un creux de 121 µm qui n'existe pas.
- **[Changement de terrain par LiDAR](../viewers/poc_lidar_terrain_change.html?lang=fr)**: Une simple soustraction fait paraître tout le bloc inchangé 0.183 m plus bas. Une fois corrigé, seul le vrai changement reste.
- **[Suivi par modèle](../viewers/poc_template_tracking.html?lang=fr)**: Même après avoir basculé sur un objet qui lui ressemble, le suivi continue de signaler « trouvé ».
- **[Profondeur par thermographie pulsée](../viewers/poc_thermography_ndt.html?lang=fr)**: Raccourcir la fenêtre d'ajustement de 25 s à 4 s ramène la pire erreur de profondeur des petits défauts de +627% à ±14%. La cause était le choix de la fenêtre, pas la physique.
- **[Tapis de Talbot et images X par réseaux](../viewers/poc_grating_interferometry.html?lang=fr)**: En absorption, le rapport contraste/bruit de la fissure n'est que de 1.34 ; en champ sombre il monte à 13.5, et en tournant le réseau on lit même la direction des fibres.
- **[Creux de quelques µm dans l'image-miroir de franges](../viewers/poc_phase_measuring_deflectometry.html?lang=fr)**: Dans une photo, le signal vaut 0.17 fois le bruit et rien n'apparaît ; dans la carte de courbure issue de l'image-miroir de franges, un creux de 1 µm dépasse le bruit de 57 fois.
- **[À quel point la surface d'un astéroïde se courbe](../viewers/poc_asteroid_curvature.html?lang=fr)**: La courbure est calculée à partir des points d'un astéroïde réel ; crêtes et bords de blocs apparaissent en couleur. La règle a d'abord été vérifiée sur une sphère dont la réponse est connue.
- **[Empilement de mise au point](../viewers/poc_focus_stacking.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier nombre d’ouverture de 2.0–16.0.
- **[Marches en interférométrie en lumière blanche](../viewers/poc_interferometry_step.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier bruit σ de 0.000–0.120.
- **[Suppression des reflets spéculaires par polarisation](../viewers/poc_polarization_specular.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier angle d’incidence de 0–80°.
- **[Contraintes en photoélasticimétrie](../viewers/poc_photoelasticity.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier angle de l’analyseur de 0–135°.
- **[Œil composé de la mouche](../viewers/poc_compound_eye.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier SNR par vue de 0.5–16.0.
- **[Vides dans la couche de jonction](../viewers/poc_ct_void_morphology.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier seuil de 0.2–0.8.
- **[Contours actifs](../viewers/poc_active_contours.html?lang=fr)**: Réexécute la fonction du PoC en faisant varier itérations de 0–1000.

## Ce qu'il permet de faire

97 sujets, chacun avec une page d'explication (utilisation et limites) et des liens vers des exemples exécutables.

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**Trouver**

- Découper des régions, les sélectionner et les compter: [explication](../../capabilities/blob-and-region.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_cell_counting](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_cell_counting.py) · [poc_particle_sizing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_particle_sizing.py) · [poc_real_coin_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py) · [poc_dense_bubble_count](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dense_bubble_count.py)
- Séparer culture et adventices dans une image RVB de champ par la couleur et la texture, puis classer le taux de couverture des adventices selon des seuils déclarés à l'avance (sans réseau appris): [explication](../../capabilities/crop-weed-cover-grade.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_weed_rice_ratio](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_weed_rice_ratio.py)
- Séparer épaisseur et matériau avec deux énergies de rayons X (bi-énergie et comptage de photons : spectre, fenêtres d'énergie, décomposition en base, numéro atomique effectif): [explication](../../capabilities/dual-energy-xray-material-discrimination.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_dual_energy_xray](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dual_energy_xray.py)
- Corriger le texte contenu dans une image par rapport à la chaîne attendue: [explication](../../capabilities/fix-text-in-images.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [fix_text_in_image](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/fix_text_in_image.py) · [poc_glyph_typo_detection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_glyph_typo_detection.py)
- Signaler ce qui diffère de pièces bonnes alignées (modèle de variation par pixel, taux de fausses alarmes déclaré): [explication](../../capabilities/good-sample-variation-model.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_good_sample_variation_model](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_good_sample_variation_model.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- Prétraitement pour l'OCR (correction de l'inclinaison, binarisation locale, découpage des lignes): [explication](../../capabilities/ocr-preprocessing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [gallery2d_segmentation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_segmentation.py) · [poc_matrix_code_reading](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_matrix_code_reading.py) · [gallery2d_region](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_region.py)
- Apprendre des seules bonnes pièces et désigner les patchs qui s'en écartent (caractéristiques classiques : loi normale par position ou banque mémoire, point de fonctionnement à rappel garanti): [explication](../../capabilities/patch-feature-anomaly-detection.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_patch_anomaly](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_patch_anomaly.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- Distinguer, par la PSF de diffusion des porteurs et le type de défaut, pourquoi une rainure fine n'est plus visible sur une image PL (inspection PL/EL des plaquettes de Si et des cellules solaires): [explication](../../capabilities/pl-carrier-diffusion-visibility.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility.py)
- Détecter de petites cibles ponctuelles et en déterminer la position au niveau sous-pixel: [explication](../../capabilities/point-target-detection.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py) · [poc_astro_photometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_astro_photometry.py)
- Appariement de gabarits robuste à l'éclairage inégal, aux reflets et aux occlusions (comparer le sens de la variation de luminosité comme un code, refuser les gabarits indéterminés): [explication](../../capabilities/robust-code-matching.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_code_matching_breakdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_code_matching_breakdown.py)
- Détecter les vides d'un collage hybride par la phase de l'écho ultrasonore et non par sa luminosité (C-scans au microscope acoustique à balayage, compromis sur la fréquence, POD): [explication](../../capabilities/sam-bond-voids.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids.py)
- Défauts de relief de surface, de la conception de l'éclairage à la détection: [explication](../../capabilities/surface-defects-with-lighting.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [illumination_design_demo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/illumination_design_demo.py) · [photometric_stereo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/photometric_stereo.py) · [poc_bump_coplanarity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bump_coplanarity.py)
- Positionnement (suivi de la ligne de mesure par appariement de gabarit): [explication](../../capabilities/template-alignment.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_template_tracking](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_template_tracking.py) · [gallery2d_contour_measure](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_contour_measure.py) · [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py)

**Mesurer**

- Estimer la matrice intrinsèque K à partir de plusieurs vues d'une cible plane (méthode de Zhang): [explication](../../capabilities/camera-intrinsics-calibration.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [camera_intrinsics_calibration](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/camera_intrinsics_calibration.py)
- Voir le plan complexe comme une « surface » (coloration de phase, bassins d'attraction, temps d'échappement, écoulement autour d'une aile): [explication](../../capabilities/complex-plane-fields.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_complex_plane_fields](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_complex_plane_fields.py)
- Mesurer en forme fermée la distance à laquelle un motif périodique se replie sur un réseau d'échantillonnage, et le temps de collision faussé par les franges (yeux composés et caméras d'inspection): [explication](../../capabilities/compound-eye-aliasing-and-ttc.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye.py)
- Remesurer les valeurs de la fiche technique d'une caméra selon la procédure EMVA 1288 et observer dans quelle mesure le prétraitement interne à la caméra les déplace (Linear et General): [explication](../../capabilities/emva1288-camera-datasheets.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_emva1288_sensor](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_sensor.py) · [poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing.py)
- Estimer les coefficients de distorsion à partir de lignes censées être droites (plumb-line, sans damier): [explication](../../capabilities/estimate-lens-distortion.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [estimate_lens_distortion](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/estimate_lens_distortion.py)
- Ptychographie de Fourier (éclairage LED oblique, récupération de phase vers amplitude et phase à grande ouverture, refus sans convergence): [explication](../../capabilities/fourier-ptychography.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_fourier_ptychography](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fourier_ptychography.py)
- Placer les mesures sur le globe terrestre (ECEF, références de hauteur, ENU local): [explication](../../capabilities/geodetic-frames.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_geodetic_height_frames](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_geodetic_height_frames.py) · [poc_geodetic_benchmarks_real](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_geodetic_benchmarks_real.py) · [dem_geodesy_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/dem_geodesy_tour.py)
- Interférométrie à réseaux (Talbot / Talbot-Lau) : transmission, phase différentielle et champ sombre à partir d'un seul balayage par pas de phase: [explication](../../capabilities/grating-interferometry.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_grating_interferometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_grating_interferometry.py)
- OCT industrielle (spectre d'interférence → linéarisation en k → compensation de dispersion → A-scans / B-scans → épaisseur des couches par l'indice de groupe): [explication](../../capabilities/industrial-oct.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_industrial_oct](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_industrial_oct.py)
- Mesurer le diamètre intérieur d'une pièce circulaire jusqu'au mm: [explication](../../capabilities/inner-diameter-in-mm.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [example_inner_diameter_mm](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/example_inner_diameter_mm.py) · [poc_dimensional_inspection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dimensional_inspection.py) · [poc_real_coin_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py)
- Temps de vol indirect (par phase) : déroulement multifréquence et modes de défaillance (repliement, trajets multiples, pixels de bord mélangés, harmoniques, lumière ambiante): [explication](../../capabilities/itof-multifreq.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_itof_multifreq](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_itof_multifreq.py)
- Le plancher d'erreur de la coupe lumineuse laser est fixé par le speckle (imagerie cohérente, statistiques du speckle, incertitude de triangulation): [explication](../../capabilities/laser-speckle-floor.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_laser_line_speckle_floor](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor.py)
- Prédire avant la prise de vue de combien les interréflexions dans les creux faussent la projection de franges et le ToF (transport global de la lumière, séparation direct/global, décalage de phase micro): [explication](../../capabilities/light-transport.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_interreflection_fringe_error](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_interreflection_fringe_error.py)
- Démodulation lock-in par pixel (imagerie à corrélation temporelle : amplitude, phase et continu ; normales d'éclairage annulaire, séparation de lumière codée, ondes thermiques): [explication](../../capabilities/lockin-demodulation.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_lockin_imaging](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lockin_imaging.py)
- Quelle part de ce chiffre revient à la méthode de mesure (R&R du système de mesure et incertitude de mesure): [explication](../../capabilities/measurement-system-and-uncertainty.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_measurement_system_analysis](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_measurement_system_analysis.py)
- Alignement par moiré (deux réseaux de pas légèrement différents, phase des franges, décalage en nm, paires différentielles et distance de Talbot): [explication](../../capabilities/moire-alignment.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_moire_alignment](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_moire_alignment.py)
- Mesurer des creux de l'ordre du µm sur une surface brillante grâce à l'image miroir de franges (déflectométrie à mesure de phase : franges → phase → pente → courbure): [explication](../../capabilities/phase-measuring-deflectometry.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_phase_measuring_deflectometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_phase_measuring_deflectometry.py)
- Mesurer les contraintes par photoélasticité (champ de contraintes, franges, images de polarisation, retard et axe): [explication](../../capabilities/photoelasticity.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_photoelasticity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_photoelasticity.py)
- Calculer la planéité à partir d'un nuage de points 3-D: [explication](../../capabilities/planarity-from-point-cloud.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [geometry_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/geometry_metrology.py) · [poc_bump_coplanarity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bump_coplanarity.py) · [poc_scan_to_bim_asbuilt](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_scan_to_bim_asbuilt.py)
- À partir de quelle taille un défaut est-il détecté (probabilité de détection POD et a90/95, MIL-HDBK-1823A): [explication](../../capabilities/probability-of-detection.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_pod_lighting](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_pod_lighting.py)
- Une ligne moyenne que ni les rayures ni les pics ne peuvent tirer (régression gaussienne robuste et filtre spline, profil et surface): [explication](../../capabilities/robust-gaussian-spline-profile-filter.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_robust_profile_filter](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_robust_profile_filter.py) · [example_robust_profile_filter](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/example_robust_profile_filter.py)
- Détecter les rayures fines et mesurer leur largeur: [explication](../../capabilities/scratch-detection-and-width.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [example_scratch_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/example_scratch_width.py) · [poc_crack_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_crack_width.py) · [poc_solar_el_inspection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_solar_el_inspection.py)
- Mesurer, avec vérité terrain, de combien le bord brillant d'un SEM grossit les dimensions: [explication](../../capabilities/sem-edge-effect-metrology.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_sem_wafer_defect_edge_bias](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_sem_wafer_defect_edge_bias.py)

- Indicateurs de conception optique séquentielle (surface de lentille idéale, énergie encerclée, FTM tangentielle et sagittale, FTM à travers le foyer): [explication](../../capabilities/sequential-lens-design-metrics.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [optical_design_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/optical_design_metrics.py) · [poc_zoom_configurations](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_zoom_configurations.py) · [poc_lens_global_search](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_global_search.py) · [poc_lens_merit_operands](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_merit_operands.py) · [poc_lens_solves_afocal](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_solves_afocal.py)
- Mesurer la forme de pièces noires brillantes et de verre par polarisation (reconstruction de forme à partir de la polarisation, ensemble d'ambiguïtés, estimation de l'indice de réfraction): [explication](../../capabilities/shape-from-polarization.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_shape_from_polarization](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_shape_from_polarization.py)

**Lumière et couleur**

- Capteur de déplacement confocal chromatique (dispersion du verre optique → table de calibrage → spectre de retour → hauteur et épaisseur): [explication](../../capabilities/chromatic-confocal-sensor.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_chromatic_confocal](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_chromatic_confocal.py)
- Mesurer la couleur (XYZ / Lab / écart de couleur): [explication](../../capabilities/colour-and-delta-e.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_white_balance](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_white_balance.py) · [poc_pigment_unmixing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_pigment_unmixing.py)
- Calculer réflexions multiples, diffusions multiples et itérations jusqu'à la convergence — en rendant la preuve de convergence et l'erreur résiduelle, et sans valeur si elle ne converge pas: [explication](../../capabilities/convergent-forward-models.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [converge_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/converge_tour.py)
- Fibres optiques et guides de lumière (dispersion du verre → modes LP → diamètre de mode → efficacité de l'éclairage): [explication](../../capabilities/fiber-and-light-guide.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_fiber_light_guide](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fiber_light_guide.py)
- Spectromètre à réseau et éclairage de microscope (largeur de bande, smile/keystone, Köhler/critique, demi-miroir coaxial): [explication](../../capabilities/grating-spectrometer-and-illumination.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_spectrometer_illumination](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_spectrometer_illumination.py)
- Faire apparaître ou effacer les petites inclinaisons d'une surface spéculaire avec le cône d'éclairage et l'ON d'observation (intersection de calottes sphériques → bandes de pente → impacts, rayures, surface satinée): [explication](../../capabilities/illumination-cone-vs-na.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_illumination_cone_vs_na](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na.py)
- Compter l'interréflexion dans les pièces concaves jusqu'à la convergence sans perdre la vérité photométrique (facteurs de forme 3D, radiosité progressive, rendu d'inspection avec réflexions): [explication](../../capabilities/interreflection-to-convergence.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_pocket_interreflection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_pocket_interreflection.py)
- Fichiers de conception optique (lecture et écriture de prescriptions séquentielles .zmx, inventaire des mots-clés, import fail-closed): [explication](../../capabilities/lens-prescription-files.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_lens_file_roundtrip](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_file_roundtrip.py)
- Consulter les propriétés optiques des matériaux dans une seule table (83 verres optiques, cristaux, semi-conducteurs, métaux, liquides et plastiques, avec coefficients et tables numériques issus de la littérature primaire, et point d'entrée pour importer un catalogue de verres): [explication](../../capabilities/material-optical-property-library.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_material_optical_library](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_material_optical_library.py)
- Trier par hyperspectral proche infrarouge des corps étrangers d'apparence identique (bandes d'absorption de la table des matériaux → réflectance de la couche diffusante → nombre d'électrons par bande de l'InGaAs): [explication](../../capabilities/nir-hyperspectral-sorting.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_hyperspectral_foreign_matter](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_hyperspectral_foreign_matter.py)
- Optique non coaxiale (miroirs de repliement, lames semi-réfléchissantes, lames inclinées, Scheimpflug et images fantômes): [explication](../../capabilities/noncoaxial-optics.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_noncoaxial_optics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_noncoaxial_optics.py)
- Calculer la réflexion, la réfraction et les interférences de la lumière: [explication](../../capabilities/optics-and-materials.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [glass_and_mirror_optics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/glass_and_mirror_optics.py) · [appearance_structural_colour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/appearance_structural_colour.py)
- Imagerie par décalage de pixel (toutes les couleurs avec des décalages d'1 pixel, suppression du repliement avec des décalages d'un demi-pixel, jusqu'aux conditions de défaillance): [explication](../../capabilities/pixel-shift-imaging.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_pixel_shift](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_pixel_shift.py)
- Supprimer la réflexion spéculaire avec quatre orientations de polarisation (séparation diélectriques et métaux): [explication](../../capabilities/polarisation-specular-removal.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [example_polarization_metal](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/example_polarization_metal.py) · [poc_polarization_specular](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_polarization_specular.py) · [specular_photometric](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/specular_photometric.py)
- Lire une trame brute de caméra polarimétrique en Stokes, DoLP et Mueller: [explication](../../capabilities/polarization-imaging.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [polarization_camera_pipeline](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/polarization_camera_pipeline.py) · [poc_polarization_specular](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_polarization_specular.py)
- Transformer une trame brute Bayer en image affichable par une formule explicable étape par étape: [explication](../../capabilities/raw-to-display-isp.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [raw_to_display_isp](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/raw_to_display_isp.py)
- La chaîne spectrale source lumineuse × objet × capteur (Planck, CIE A, LED, filtres, QE du Si, produits et intégrales de courbes): [explication](../../capabilities/spectral-radiometry-chain.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_nir_silicon_transmission](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_nir_silicon_transmission.py)
- Caméras linéaires TDI et objectifs pour caméra linéaire (nombre d'étages et SNR, FTM du défaut de synchronisation, éclairement et FTM selon la hauteur d'image): [explication](../../capabilities/tdi-linescan-and-lens.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_tdi_linescan](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_tdi_linescan.py)
- Mesure de l'épaisseur d'une couche mince par réflectométrie spectrale (matrice de transfert → couleur d'interférence → FFT, ajustement de modèle, cartes d'épaisseur): [explication](../../capabilities/thin-film-reflectometry.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_thin_film_reflectometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_thin_film_reflectometry.py)
- L'eau dans les images d'inspection (caustiques au fond d'un bassin, une bouteille vue de côté avec son niveau, une surface mouillée, une coque sous l'eau): [explication](../../capabilities/water-inspection-images.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_water_inspection_images](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_water_inspection_images.py)
- L'eau et les milieux transparents (vagues, Fresnel et réfraction à la surface, couleur et diffusion sous l'eau, une bouteille comme lentille): [explication](../../capabilities/water-transparent-media.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_water_transparent_media](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_water_transparent_media.py)

**Ondes et signaux**

- Mesurer la direction avec un réseau, et séparer distance et vitesse: [explication](../../capabilities/beamforming-and-range-doppler.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_multibeam_bathymetry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_multibeam_bathymetry.py) · [poc_bev_sensor_fusion](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bev_sensor_fusion.py)
- Un seul battement (modes de membrane, franges d'interférence, ordres de diffraction, moiré d'impression, et transformation du son en encre): [explication](../../capabilities/beats-fringes-and-screens.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_beats_fringes_and_screens](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_beats_fringes_and_screens.py)
- Concevoir en forme fermée les cavités d'interféromètre, le budget de bruit quantique et une forme d'onde connue dans un bruit coloré (les équations du télescope à ondes gravitationnelles appliquées aux interféromètres d'inspection, aux vibromètres et à l'AE): [explication](../../capabilities/gw-interferometer-quantum-noise-and-matched-filter.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_gw_detector_pseudo_simulation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_gw_detector_pseudo_simulation.py)
- Détecter les défauts périodiques dans un signal 1-D: [explication](../../capabilities/periodic-defects-in-1d-signals.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_web_roll_periodicity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_web_roll_periodicity.py) · [poc_rail_corrugation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_rail_corrugation.py) · [poc_bearing_diagnosis](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bearing_diagnosis.py)
- Diagnostiquer des anomalies à partir des vibrations et du son: [explication](../../capabilities/vibration-and-acoustics.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_bearing_diagnosis](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bearing_diagnosis.py) · [poc_rail_corrugation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_rail_corrugation.py)

**Reconstruire**

- Corriger la distorsion de l'objectif sur l'image entière (en barillet, en coussinet, tangentielle): [explication](../../capabilities/lens-distortion-correction.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [lens_undistort](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/lens_undistort.py)
- Reconstruire des coupes à partir de projections (CT): [explication](../../capabilities/tomography-reconstruction.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_ct_fidelity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ct_fidelity.py) · [poc_ct_void_morphology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ct_void_morphology.py)
- Sculpter un solide à partir de silhouettes (intersection de volumes visuels): [explication](../../capabilities/visual-hull-from-silhouettes.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [space_carving](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/space_carving.py) · [poc_livestock_body_volume](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_livestock_body_volume.py)

**Construire un flux de travail**

- Aligner et superposer: [explication](../../capabilities/align-and-stack.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_astro_photometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_astro_photometry.py) · [poc_registration_basin](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_registration_basin.py)
- Comparer à une image de référence (golden), mesurer les défauts et juger lot par lot: [explication](../../capabilities/golden-compare.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [golden_compare](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/golden_compare.py)
- Valider une recette d'inspection et une spécification sur des jeux connus de pièces bonnes et défectueuses avant le déploiement, et mesurer les marges: [explication](../../capabilities/inspection-fixture.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [inspection_fixture](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_fixture.py)
- Inspecter un dossier en une seule fois, juger selon une spécification, puis produire agrégation, SPC, rapport et journal d'audit: [explication](../../capabilities/inspection-workflow.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [inspection_workflow](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_workflow.py)
- Budgéter les tolérances de fabrication d'un objectif selon la FTM et le rendement: [explication](../../capabilities/lens-tolerancing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_lens_tolerancing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_tolerancing.py)
- Ne calculer qu'une fois la validation, les voisinages et les noyaux de transformation: [explication](../../capabilities/reuse-computation-results.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [perf_pointcloud_descriptors](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_pointcloud_descriptors.py) · [perf_monogenic_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_monogenic_metrics.py) · [perf_prepared_volume_probe](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_prepared_volume_probe.py) · [perf_polarization_analyze](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_polarization_analyze.py) · [perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream.py)
- Écrire en JSON les valeurs de retour typées d'un op et les relire à l'identique bit à bit: [explication](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-json.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [typed_results_json](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_json.py)
- Rendre lisibles en Markdown les valeurs de retour typées d'un op, en y intégrant du JSON pour la relecture: [explication](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-markdown.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [typed_results_markdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_markdown.py)
- Exporter des résultats d'inspection typés dans un rapport Excel (.xlsx): [explication](../../capabilities/xlsx-report.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [xlsx_report](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/xlsx_report.py)

**Afficher**

- Transformer les résultats en figures lisibles par l'humain: [explication](../../capabilities/figures-and-annotation.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_colormap_readability](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_colormap_readability.py) · [poc_dem_terrain](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dem_terrain.py)
- Transformer les résultats en une page autonome à manipuler (onglets, badges, 3-D, vidéo, langues): [explication](../../capabilities/interactive-poc-viewer.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [interactive_poc_viewer](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/interactive_poc_viewer.py) · [interactive_anim3d_viewer](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/interactive_anim3d_viewer.py)
- Tracer des lignes et des régions sans connaître la couleur du fond (couleur inversée): [explication](../../capabilities/inverted-colour-overlays.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [annotate_paper_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/annotate_paper_tour.py)
- Dessiner directement un nuage de points en image couleur (empreintes sous-pixel, composition triée par profondeur, addition conservant la quantité de lumière): [explication](../../capabilities/point-cloud-to-picture.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [points_render_basics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/points_render_basics.py) · [poc_flybrain_pointcloud](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_flybrain_pointcloud.py)
- Placer du texte et des tableaux à l'endroit souhaité sur une image: [explication](../../capabilities/text-and-tables-on-images.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [annotate_paper_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/annotate_paper_tour.py)

**Dessiner**

- Transformer une photographie en une seule ligne (pointillé, circuit, cercles en rotation): [explication](../../capabilities/one-stroke-drawing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_one_stroke_epicycles](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_one_stroke_epicycles.py)
- Dessins de systèmes optiques (coupes de lentilles, schémas d'implantation de systèmes d'imagerie et dessins de fabrication de lentilles simples en SVG / DXF): [explication](../../capabilities/optics-drawings.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_optics_drawings](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_optics_drawings.py)
- Photographier une scène 3-D à travers la prescription réelle de l'objectif (profondeur de champ, bokeh en œil de chat, éclairement des coins, franges colorées, images fantômes): [explication](../../capabilities/photograph-through-prescription.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_photograph_through_prescription](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_photograph_through_prescription.py)
- Créer des images qui trompent l'œil et noter le système de mesure (illusions d'optique, dessin infini, vidéos en boucle): [explication](../../capabilities/pictures-that-carry-their-own-truth.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_illusions_and_perpetual_drawing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_illusions_and_perpetual_drawing.py)
- Caméra plénoptique fondée sur une prescription optique: [explication](../../capabilities/plenoptic-camera.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_plenoptic_camera](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_plenoptic_camera.py)
- Des figures où un théorème fait barrière (Apollonius, Ford, dôme géodésique, phyllotaxie, IFS, courbes remplissant l'espace): [explication](../../capabilities/theorems-as-pictures.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_theorems_as_pictures](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_theorems_as_pictures.py)

</div>

## Ce que l'on voit et la valeur mesurée

Chaque valeur est mesurée par rapport à la vérité terrain que chaque PoC a elle-même définie (forme close, solution analytique ou valeur publiée) ; l'exécution du PoC affiche la même valeur.

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**Inspection d'images et mesure d'aspect**

- [Limite de détection des rayures fines](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_defect_floor/02_defect_floor_sweep.gif) (GIF): Le même défaut, de plus en plus marqué, est inséré dans un fond réel (briques) et dans un fond synthétique de niveau de bruit identique. **Même à niveau de bruit égal, la limite de détection sur le fond réel est de 2.03 à 3.47 fois celle du fond synthétique (pour des défauts de position et d'amplitude connues).** [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_defect_floor.py)
- [Contours actifs](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_active_contours/06_u_shape_snakes.mp4) (vidéo): Dans l'encoche en U, le snake classique (rouge) n'entre pas, tandis que le GVF (bleu) atteint le fond. Le vert est le vrai contour. **En remplaçant uniquement la force externe par le GVF, on obtient un Dice de 0.993 (par rapport au vrai contour).** [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_active_contours.py)
- [Déformation par DIC](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dic_strain/05_tensile_ramp.mp4) (vidéo): On lit la carte des déformations à partir d'images de speckle tout en augmentant la charge d'un essai de traction (la machine d'essai tourne en même temps de 2 degrés). **La vraie déformation est de 3000 µε. La petite déformation donne 2341 µε, sous-estimée à cause de la rotation, tandis que Green-Lagrange donne 2961 µε (théorie : 3005 µε).** [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dic_strain.py)
- [Code-barres 1-D](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_barcode_1d/03_collapse.png) (figure): A home-made simple code (not a real standard) damaged four ways, with success, misread and unreadable counted as three separate outcomes. Over the 384 images past the onset of damage, the strict decoder that checks run structure misreads 7.3 %, while the lenient decoder that always returns nine digits misreads 46.1 % — and has the higher success rate (47.7 % versus 40.1 %). The tilt cliff is pure geometry (predicted 15.95 degrees) and lands between 15 and 16 degrees. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_barcode_1d.py)
- [Diagnostic d'anomalies de roulements](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bearing_diagnosis/03_sk_bands.png) (figure): An impulse train synthesised at the closed-form defect frequency (BPFO 104.556 Hz), sunk into noise, with detection rates of the raw spectrum and the envelope spectrum side by side. The worst SNR at which 10 of 10 seeds still detect is -0.9 dB for the raw spectrum and -18.4 dB for the envelope, a 17.5 dB gap. But a record with no defect at all still produces a global prominence of 43; had the threshold not been set from the null recording, this PoC would have been its own false positive. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bearing_diagnosis.py)
- [Asymétrie bilatérale](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bilateral_asymmetry/02_gain_vs_amplitude.png) (figure): A perfectly symmetric synthetic skull with a known bulge added on one side, mirrored and overlaid. Even a perfectly symmetric specimen never scores 0: the floor drops from 1.33 mm (point-to-point) to 0.030 mm (point-to-plane) to 0.012 mm (neighbourhood smoothing). The residual-minimising plane is dragged 2.92 mm / 1.72 degrees by a 6.33 mm bulge, and 46 % of the asymmetry disappears. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bilateral_asymmetry.py)
- [Largeur des fissures](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_crack_width/01_width_sweep.png) (figure): Cracks from 0.05 to 2.0 mm wide in a field where 1 px = 0.20 mm, measured by thresholding-and-counting and by integrating the intensity deficit across the crack. Thresholding returns nothing at or below 0.20 mm and reports 0.200 mm for all four true widths between 0.25 and 0.40 mm. Integration tracks continuously down to 0.05 mm (0.25 px), but curved illumination adds a +0.1741 mm offset under a first-order baseline (+0.0062 mm with second order). [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_crack_width.py)
- [Contrôle dimensionnel de pièces](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dimensional_inspection/02_blur_cliff.png) (figure): A part defined by a signed distance function (slot width 50.50 px, 1 px = 12.5 µm), imaged through a known PSF and noise and measured by four caliper systems. Otsu's integer width (the null) has an RMS error of 0.464 px = 5.8 µm; the buried 1-D measuring implementation is biased by -0.0113 px = -0.14 µm, 41x better. Once the edge spacing drops below 3.09 PSF widths the width comes out systematically large, and the API keeps returning success. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dimensional_inspection.py)
- [Défauts de tissu](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fabric_defect/04_scene.png) (figure): Three defect classes (thread break, stain, dim patch) buried in a weave of period 8 px, with the detector's score map and per-class ROC curves side by side. The everyday recipe 'notch out the grid, remove low frequencies' pools to an AUC of 0.8113 and looks like a pass, but the dim-patch class alone scores 0.4746, worse than chance. The one line that removes low frequencies was deleting the defect along with the lighting; drop it and the class recovers to 0.9998. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fabric_defect.py)
- [Orientation des fibres](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fiber_orientation/01_scene.png) (figure): Orientation of 140 fibres drawn from a von Mises distribution, read with a structure tensor. For a true mean of 177.9 degrees the arithmetic mean reports 105.55 degrees (-72.33), while the doubled-angle circular mean is off by +0.17 degrees — with not one bit of the image or the measurement changed. Weighting every pixel equally drops the orientation index by -31.3 %. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fiber_orientation.py)
- [Profil de denture des engrenages](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_gear_tooth_metrology/03_spectrum.png) (figure): Eccentricity and tooth profile read from a gear drawn with the closed-form involute. The null's least-squares circle has a diameter of 47.278 mm, which is neither the pitch (48), tip (52) nor root (43) circle. One missing tooth turns an eccentricity of 0.050 mm into 0.1285 mm (+157 %); the traditional method of one sample per tooth returns 0.0501 mm (+0.2 %). [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_gear_tooth_metrology.py)
- [Lecture de codes binaires 2-D](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_matrix_code_reading/01_symbol_and_errors.png) (figure): A code with QR-style layout and random data bits (no error correction), damaged by blur, tilt and occlusion, with the raw bit error rate counted directly. The nulls sit at chance (0.526 / 0.507) while the reader scores 0.0000. Cliffs: 78 degrees of tilt, two modules of finder-pattern occlusion; run beside the condition that receives the true homography, localisation is always the stage that fails first. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_matrix_code_reading.py)
- [Moiré et mura](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_screen/04_moire_scene.png) (figure): Moire from the interference of the display's stripes with the camera's pixel grid, overlaid on genuine luminance non-uniformity in one image. At a smoothing σ of 8 px the total error is +0.9 %, made of +8.3 % moire leakage cancelling -7.4 % attenuation of the real defect. At k = 0.67 the fundamental beat looks safe, yet the third harmonic lands in the defect band and the leakage reaches +202.6 % of the true value. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_moire_screen.py)
- [Distribution granulométrique](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_particle_sizing/02_density_sweep.png) (figure): D10 / D50 / D90 from a synthetic scatter of particles, with merges (pulling large) and edge cuts (pulling small) counted separately. At an area fraction of 13.8 % the D50 error is +0.55 % — because 28 merges and 19 edge cuts happen to balance. Number- and area-weighting give D50 values of 26.9 and 44.7 µm (1.66x) from the same blobs. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_particle_sizing.py)
- [Déformation de fluage](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_strain_history/05_history_video.gif) (GIF): One hour of creep in 25 frames, with strain history from accumulating adjacent-frame displacements versus comparing each frame directly with the reference. At the end, cumulative errs by 61 µε and direct by 1878 µε — cumulative wins 31x and the textbook crossover in time never appears (it lives on the noise axis instead). Thinning from 24 to 4 steps worsens cumulative from -60 to -606 µε; what matters is the deformation per step, not the number of steps. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_strain_history.py)
- [Rugosité de surface](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_roughness/04_lambda_c_sweep.png) (figure): A surface synthesised from a prescribed PSD (so the true Sq follows analytically from Parseval), with tilt, waviness, lay and scratches added before the roughness parameters are measured. Calling the raw rms 'Sq' overstates it 20x; removing only the plane still leaves 1.8x. At 8 µm sampling Sa is -3.5 % (pass) while Sz is -19.8 % (fail); Sz keeps growing with evaluation area and never plateaus, so there is no such thing as the true Sz. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_surface_roughness.py)
- [Dérive thermique de la caméra](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermal_drift_metrology/04_error_maps.png) (figure): A 40 mm square measured by a camera whose focal length, mount and principal point drift with temperature ΔT, the error split into a + b·R in image radius. At ΔT = 15 K the constant term is +224.5 ppm (the single-cause control gives +225.3 ppm). Drift rises above the noise floor (23 ppm for a 25-frame average) only from ΔT = 1.6 K; below that, the honest report is that no temperature effect is visible. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_thermal_drift_metrology.py)
- [Lumière parasite et contraste](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_veiling_glare/03_mtf_curves.png) (figure): A PSF whose core stays sharp while only its tail is made heavier, with the slanted-edge MTF and the black level of a dark square measured on the same image. Raising the tail fraction from 0 to 0.20 moves MTF50 from 0.2347 to 0.2249 cyc/px (-4.2 %, still passing) while the black level goes from 0.0 to 15.7 % (failing). A ±16 px measurement window captures only 6 % of the tail's energy; a glare figure means nothing until the measurement extent is declared. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_veiling_glare.py)
- [Profil en coupe du cordon de soudure](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_profile/01_laser_images.png) (figure): The profile h(x) recovered from the row position of a single laser line, with reinforcement height, width and undercut read off it. The centroid beats the null (integer argmax per column) by 9.3x, and with zero noise the log-parabola is exact to machine precision, 12 orders better than a plain parabola; at 1 % noise the two are 0.00272 versus 0.00260 mm, indistinguishable. Five spatter points break all three estimators together to 0.11 mm (40x); what matters is not refinement but which peak you pick. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_weld_bead_profile.py)
- [Détection de fuites par le son](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_leak_localization/04_correlation_zoom.png) (figure): Locating a leak in a 120 m buried main from the arrival-time difference at two sensors, with the source, sound speed, attenuation and reflections all planted as ground truth. With the true sound speed the estimate lands within 0.0107 m at 0 dB SNR (1.2x the Knapp-Carter bound of 0.0091 m); the threshold was predicted at -20.1 dB but measured at -12.5 dB, below which the error jumps to 36.0 m — the whole search window. Yet a 10 % error in the assumed sound speed alone moves the dig by 1.798 m (against the predicted (dc/c)(x-L/2) = 1.800 m), and on a pipe that changes from ductile iron to plastic mid-run the time difference is exactly zero, so iron, plastic or their average all miss by 18.001 m: no calibration of a single speed can fix a factor multiplying zero. The reflection prediction failed — GCC-PHAT beat the plain correlation by only 10 %, because 0.121 m of its 0.121 m RMS is bias, and whitening acts on the magnitude while the delay lives in the phase. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_leak_localization.py)
- [Fusion pour la maintenance des équipements](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_machine_condition_fusion/11_sweep_record_length.png) (figure): Six states of a rotating machine (healthy, misalignment, unbalance, outer-race spall, poor lubrication, looseness) are built from closed-form bearing defect frequencies, the exact steady fin-equation solution for the casing temperature, and a planted shaft offset, then classified from three sensors. Fused accuracy is 100.0 % — but vibration alone is also 100.0 %: thermal and geometry add nothing. Misalignment reaches 100.0 % from any single sensor (correlations with the true severity 0.843 / 0.841 / 0.978 — three faces of one number), while thermal alone can never separate healthy, unbalance and looseness (48/48 confusions inside that trio) and dropping vibration takes them from 100.0 % to 50.0 % / 31.2 %. Fusion only earns its keep once vibration breaks: at noise sigma 1.6 it lifts 45.8 % to 78.1 %. The cliffs were predicted on single features: the 0.5X order bin needs T > 2/f_r = 68.6 ms (measured d' 2.45 -> 4.32 across the 50 -> 70 ms step), the thermal spread dies past the 66 mm half-diameter (d' 0.00 at 96 mm pitch). The sideband prediction 1/T < FTF = 86.1 ms was wrong; the peak-reading window condition 1.2/FTF = 103 ms is the right one. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_machine_condition_fusion.py)
- [Taille de grain](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_metal_grain_size/08_failure_area.png) (figure): Grains are synthesized as a 2-D Voronoi tessellation with 2-px boundaries, then etching unevenness, noise and boundary gaps are added; ASTM E112 grain size G is measured by the planimetric method (Otsu + connected components) and the lineal-intercept method (local threshold + test lines in 4 directions). The planimetric method survives noise alone (-0.02) and etching unevenness alone (-0.40) but dies under both (+3.82), and loses one G step at 7.2 % boundary gaps; the intercept method holds to 40.7 % — the predicted 29.3 % was wrong because only 0.76 f of the boundary actually disappears in the mask. A duplex structure gives a whole-field G of 7.82 that matches neither the fine (9.01) nor the coarse (6.15) population; only 4 of 64 tiles fall within ±0.5 of it. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_metal_grain_size.py)
- [Tri des déchets par matériau](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_recycling_sorting/13_spectra.png) (figure): Fragments on a belt carry material, dirt, wetness, tilt and occlusion in known amounts, imaged in 64 SWIR bands. Per-fragment degradation is closed form, s(l)=g*R(l)*exp(-w*A_w(l))+(a*u(l)+c), so invariance can be predicted before measuring: the spectral angle is invariant to the multiplicative g (0.995 to 0.990 as dirt goes 0 to 0.8; 0.993 at 70 degrees of tilt), and a second derivative annihilates the linear baseline (raw SAM falls 0.995 to 0.827 under an additive baseline while the derivative stays at 0.995 at every level). Two predictions failed: wetness is largely removed by the second derivative (0.818 against 0.282 for raw SAM) because a second derivative weights Gaussian bands by 1/sigma^2 ((43/70)^2 = 0.37), and continuum removal beats raw SAM while the baseline is weak (0.983 vs 0.865) but loses once it is strong (0.736 vs 0.827). Featureless metal vanishes under differentiation (recall 0.06) and a flatness gate restores it to 0.98. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_recycling_sorting.py)
- [Profil des filetages](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_screw_thread_metrology/05_tilt_correction.png) (figure): An M6-like silhouette drawn from the ISO 68-1 basic triangle in closed form (1 px = 25 µm). An FFT of the binarised column widths reports half the true pitch, 20 px, because the two profiles are offset by P/2 and their sum is constant. Tilting the axis by 3 degrees splits the flank angles into 33.18 / 26.74 degrees: their half-sum 29.81 is the true flank angle and their half-difference 3.19 estimates the tilt. Pitch measured on one flank drifts at first order (+3.28 / -2.79 %) while the crest spacing drifts at second order (-0.12 %); undoing the tilt leaves P -0.009 % and d2 +0.033 %. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_screw_thread_metrology.py)
- [EL des cellules solaires](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_solar_el_inspection/03_scene_map.png) (figure): A crystalline-silicon cell (100 fingers, 3 busbars, 70 grains) is rendered in closed form as an EL image with two electrically isolated regions (true inactive area 4.77 %), 5 cracks and 8 finger interruptions, then imaged through cos^4 vignetting and photon noise. The zero-point global threshold reports a dark-pixel fraction of 21.7 % as the inactive area, but 42 % of those pixels are fingers and busbars and 33 % are grains and vignetting; only 20 % are truly inactive. Dividing out the grid with row/column profiles and gating each defect type separately gives 4.61 % (-0.15 points), crack recall 0.88–1.00 and 8/8 interruptions. Because sk_frangi normalises by the per-image maximum, a calibration line only fixes the scale if it is the strongest ridge in the image (a line like the real cracks responds 0.69, a 3 px black line 1.00); without calibration the defect-free cell produces 147 px of false cracks. From grain contrast c=0.24 false cracks and swallowed interruption bands start together, and the crack-width cliff at 1.25 px follows the linear width-times-depth rule (predicted 1.38 px). [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_solar_el_inspection.py)
- [AOI des soudures](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_solder_fillet_aoi/07_shift_cliff.png) (figure): A 1608 chip's pads and terminations carry circular-arc fillets fixed by contact angle and cross-section area; three ring lights at different elevations (red 30-40°, green 15-30°, blue 0-15° of surface tilt) are integrated over a GGX lobe to synthesise the AOI image, and joints are graded good / insufficient / bridge / tombstone. With an 18° contact angle the concave arc rises to 72° at the wall, so even the lowest ring sees only 28.8 % of the height (predicted): naively integrating the band tilts recovers 0.284 of the true height. Extrapolating the arc to the wall from the positions where the colour changes lands at +1.7 % ± 5.3 % for free arcs, but once extra solder pins the toe at the pad edge it drifts to -42.3 %. A 0.16 mm placement offset steepens the toe past 30°, the green band vanishes and a good joint is called insufficient although its true height has risen (predicted 0.16 mm; the truth only fails at 0.28 mm). Surface roughness, against expectation, does not move the dark edge; it breaks at roughness 0.5 together with the loss of the red band and at 0.6 the whole fillet drops below the dark threshold. The zero point (Lab distance of the pad mean colour) is 100 % right under reference conditions yet flags 56 % of good joints and 68 % of bridges once offset, roughness and lighting vary — no discrimination — while the arc-based grading scores good 98 % / insufficient 98 % / bridge 100 % / tombstone 88 %, the misses all being lifts of 8.7-11.0°. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_solder_fillet_aoi.py)
- [Déformation des murs construits](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_asbuilt_wall_deviation/02_aabb_vs_yaw.png) (figure): A 6.0 x 4.0 x 2.7 m indoor point cloud carries known defects: wall plumb errors of 1.20-9.00 mrad, a 5.00 mrad plan-view squareness error, and a 9 mm out-of-plane bulge. The naive axis-aligned bounding box over-reports the width by 19.1 mm, and rotating the room by just 1 deg relative to the scanner axes adds 80.1 mm (611.1 mm at 10 deg) — it is measuring W cos psi + D sin psi, the room's heading, while the plane-pair distance stays at +2.24 mm regardless. The AABB also grows with sample size (+14.9 to +19.7 mm for 256x more points, the 2-sigma-sqrt(2 ln N) extreme-value law), so adding measurements makes it worse. Furniture outliers break the two estimators differently: least squares is off by 11x the truth at 10 % contamination (66.3 mrad vs a closed-form 63.8), while RANSAC holds to 45 % and then jumps onto the cabinet at 55 % — where the plumb error stays a harmless 0.13 mrad but the plane itself is 449.9 mm out, so one number hides the failure. A single bulge corrupts plumb (-1.258 mrad), squareness (+0.500 mrad) and the internal dimension (+2.2 mm) at once, all predicted in closed form to within 0.06 mrad. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_asbuilt_wall_deviation.py)
- [Respiration des électrodes](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_battery_electrode_breathing/03_displacement.png) (figure): Electrode swelling is read from two cross-sections by tracking layer boundaries to sub-pixel accuracy. Ground truth: anode 1.00 %, cathode 0.20 %, separator 0 %, stack 480.0 -> 482.136 um (+2.136 um = +0.4450 %). Counting binarised pixels resolves 0 of 12 layers, while a fixed-threshold crossing is also sub-pixel and lands on the answer with no noise (+0.004495) — what kills it is the control group: with zero strain, an illumination offset of +0.10 alone yields -1.07e-02, a false strain 2.4x the true one and of the opposite sign, and a gain of x1.30 halves the crossings so the measurement fails outright. The gradient-peak estimator (measure_pos) stays at exactly 0.0 under both. Doubting our own '1.8e-14 px error' and shifting the stack by fractions of a pixel showed that figure to be an artefact of putting the truth on pixel centres: the real error is peak locking, RMS 0.0088 / max 0.0122 px, worth 3.0 % on the anode's strain. The cliff has two steps, and the one where boundary counts collapse arrives 5.5x earlier than the predicted 3-sigma limit. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_battery_electrode_breathing.py)
- [Tortuosité des électrodes](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_battery_electrode_tortuosity/02_map_transport.png) (figure): A synthetic micro-CT of a lithium-ion electrode coating, measured for porosity and tortuosity. Against the shop-floor default (Bruggeman tau = eps^-0.5) we put the real thing: the steady-state diffusion equation solved on the same volume. At eps = 0.4448 the rule gives 1.499 against a true 1.843 (-18.6 %), and sweeping eps from 0.691 to 0.168 widens the error from -8.2 % to -69.1 % (fitted exponents 1.78 and 2.70 — never 1.5). The geodesic tortuosity that imaging papers report (1.182) merely has a square that hugs Bruggeman to within 1 %; neither goes near the truth. The decisive control: hold porosity at 0.4448 vs 0.4510 and flatten the particles 4:1, and through-plane tau jumps 1.843 -> 6.794 (anisotropy 0.97 -> 4.20) while the rule returns the same 1.5 for both — in-plane looks right (-8 %), through-plane collapses (-78 %). Closed pores peak at 1.92 % and are not the culprit; the necks, 0.40 of a particle radius wide, are. We predicted a resolution cliff and found none: coarsening the voxel 5.5x moves eps by -0.4 % but tau by +72 %, with no warning of any kind. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_battery_electrode_tortuosity.py)
- [Coplanarité des bumps](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bump_coplanarity/06_absorbed_defect.png) (figure): A height map of 256 Cu pillars is planted with 50 µm PV warpage, 4 µm 1-sigma pillar-to-pillar spread and six short bumps. The zero point — subtract only a least-squares plane, the JEDEC seating plane — reads the individual deviation with 8.84 µm RMS error (2.2x the real spread), raising 58 false rejects while missing one genuinely short bump. A quadratic reference surface brings it to 1.23 µm with zero false rejects; a cubic one is worse (1.37 µm), because the planted high-order mode is quartic and the four extra terms only absorb real signal. The cliff is predictable from geometry: the quadratic fit degrades to the 4 µm level at 169.9 µm PV (predicted) versus 169.4 µm (measured). Adding a real low-order defect — the centre sagging 8 µm — removes 88.2 % of it from the residual while the short-bump detection count stays at 5 of 6, and misses rise from 0 to 2; the lost signal reappears as warpage growing 30.06 to 36.20 µm. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bump_coplanarity.py)
- [Inclinaison de la puce et TSV](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_die_tilt_tsv_overlay/04_tilt_cliff.png) (figure): Two stacked dies carry a 7x7 TSV lattice with a planted overlay of (+0.800, -0.450) um, +0.01500 deg of rotation and a die tilt of (1.20, 0.70) deg; a single CT volume is the whole measurement. Comparing the top-surface via openings directly misses by 2.419 um — 2.4x the +-1.0 um spec, and larger than the 0.918 um real overlay. The bias matches the closed form 'die thickness x lateral part of the top-surface normal' to a ratio of 0.996 / 0.999, and re-projecting each via down its own fitted axis recovers the truth to 0.0023 um. The prediction that failed: tilt fakes rotation as well as translation, through the sin(alpha)sin(beta) shear of Rx Ry — predicted +0.00733 deg against +0.00696 deg measured as the difference from the zero-tilt control. Axis correction does not remove it; only de-projecting with the measured tilt does. The cliff lands at 0.492 deg measured against 0.491 deg predicted. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_die_tilt_tsv_overlay.py)
- [Sondage multifaisceaux](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_multibeam_bathymetry/18_real_smile.png) (figure): Multibeam echosounding: get the water-column sound-speed profile wrong and a flat seafloor curls up (the classic smile / frown). Only the outer beams break — nadir is almost untouched, which is exactly the part the field checks with a bar check. The ground truth is planted here: a perfectly horizontal bottom at 50.0 m, sound speed 1520 to 1480 m/s (gradient -0.800 /s), 141 beams over +/-70 degrees, and the verdict comes from a real standard, IHO S-44 Order 1a (TVU at 50 m = 0.8201 m). The cliff was predicted twice before measuring: a rough expansion, dz = (g D^2 / 2 c0) tan^2(theta), gives 48.15 degrees, and the exact closed form (rays are circular arcs inside a constant-gradient layer) gives 48.68. The measurement with echo detection disabled lands on 48.68 — the exact form is right to 2.0e-11 m, while the expansion misses by 0.53 degrees (within 3.0 % out to 45 degrees, 19.4 % high at 70). One prediction was wrong: echo detection was expected to be a negligible floor, but the full-chain cliff is 47.95 degrees, 0.73 degrees earlier. At 70 degrees the illuminated strip stretches the echo to 21396 us (171 times the nadir value) and skews it, pulling the amplitude peak 0.725 m shallow — which is why real systems switch to phase detection on the outer beams. Controls separate the culprit: refraction alone is -2.6974 m, angle estimation contributes 0.000000 m and echo detection -0.2221 m. The textbook footprint formula is only 34 % of the truth at 70 degrees (cos^2 gives 8.21 m against a measured 24.14 m) because a steered array widens its beam as 1/cos(theta), so the correct exponent is three (cos^3 is off by -0.6 %). Discretisation alone can breach the standard: treating each cast layer as iso-velocity puts +1.2994 m on a 65-degree beam with two layers, converging first order to +0.0785 m with 32. The only check available without truth is the overlap between adjacent lines: 2.697 m of disagreement at the edge (3.3 times TVU) but exactly 0.0000 m in the middle of the overlap, where both lines use the same beam angle and the errors cancel — so it is invisible unless the full swath edge is compared. With upward refraction the outer beams never reach bottom: nothing is recorded rather than recorded wrongly (critical angle predicted at 73.90 degrees, bracketed by the last beam that arrives at 73.0 and the first that does not at 74.0). Across a 200-case grid of 25 sound-speed differences and 8 depths, 78.0 % of +/-65-degree swaths fail Order 1a at the edge. Finally, a one-way documentation hole was closed on the way: the vector-form refract only told callers to loop one ray at a time, never mentioning that refract_rays already returns a per-ray TIR mask. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_multibeam_bathymetry.py)
- [Défaut de repérage d'impression](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_print_registration/01_plates.png) (figure): Measuring print misregistration from the printed sheet. Four CMYK plates carry the conventional screen angles (C 15, M 75, Y 0, K 45 degrees) at 133 lpi scanned at 1200 dpi, giving a screen pitch of 9.02 px, and each plate is displaced by a known amount. The central claim is geometry: a halftone is a lattice, so correlation recovers not the displacement d but d modulo the screen lattice. Along the lattice axes that limit is p/2 = 4.51 px and along the diagonal p/sqrt(2) = 6.38 px; beyond it the measurement folds back. Stated in closed form before measuring, it matches at 144 of 148 points (four plates by 37 sweep positions) to within 0.1096 px. The four exceptions are ties on the boundary of the fundamental cell, with at most 0.165 px of margin, and reducing modulo the lattice makes every point agree: the estimator is not wrong, it returns one of the lattice-equivalent answers, and two independent estimators fold identically. The consequence is concrete. A true displacement of 9.64 px, 4.8 times the 2.0 px tolerance, appears as 1.09 to 3.32 px depending only on the screen angle, so two of the four plates look like they pass — from one sheet displaced by one amount. The null baseline, the centroid of ink, does not fold but loses its scale: the slope follows the closed form k = 1 - beta, where beta is the ink of the flat background over the total ink, predicted 0.3983 against 0.3711 measured. One effect was not predicted at all: a 0.9864 px ripple at the screen pitch, caused by rows of dots entering and leaving at the window edge, which a tapered-window control drops to 0.0048 px. Switching to an FM (stochastic) screen removes the folding entirely, with an error of at most 0.0029 px across the whole sweep, which places the blame on the periodicity of the AM screen rather than on the estimator, at the cost of 0.91 times the contrast. A window containing a registration mark reads correctly to 0.0823 px, but with 0.600 % paper stretch and 0.120 degrees of plate rotation the error grows linearly with distance at 0.006319 px per px and exceeds tolerance from 316.5 px predicted, 322.5 px measured — 44 % of the sheet. Two rulers reverse the ranking: raw correlation is the most accurate at 0.0071 px yet agrees with the pass/fail verdict only 67.6 % of the time, while low-pass filtering agrees 89.2 % of the time at 94 times the error; a two-stage estimator that picks the representative with the blunt method and refines with the sharp one wins both, and it fails exactly at the points where the coarse error exceeded the cell radius of 4.511 px. The synthesiser itself was a trap: at 150 lpi and 1200 dpi the pitch is exactly 8.00 px, every dot samples in phase, and the centroid jumps 1.4 px for each 0.5 px of displacement — avoiding the integer ratio is the fix, not supersampling. Finally, an operator used outside its domain failed confidently: fs.frame_align returned inlier_ratio 1.00 and rms_px 0.645 while missing the true (0.00, +1.30) by 80.85 px, and the answer was not even a lattice vector. That finding was fixed in the operator: its docstring now records the measurement, and the vote histogram's runner-up over winner is returned as vote_margin — 1.000 on this halftone against 0.143 on a star field, where the agreement ratio is 1.00 in both. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_print_registration.py)
- [Usure ondulatoire des rails](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_rail_corrugation/03_transfer_zoom.png) (figure): Reading track irregularity with a chord (versine) — the distance from the mid-point to a chord drawn between two points, still the working method in track recording and in acceptance of rail re-profiling. The null baseline, reading the versine directly as height, ranges from 0.0 % to 200.0 % of the truth depending on wavelength: one and the same 10 m chord reads lambda=10 m at +100 %, lambda=1.5 m at +50 %, lambda=30 m at -50 %, and lambda=5.0 / 2.5 / 1.0 m at -100 %. Over-reading and under-reading happen at once, so no single correction factor repairs it. The blind spots are exact geometry: |H(lambda)| = |1 - cos(pi L / lambda)| is exactly zero at lambda = L/(2n) and doubles at lambda = L/(2n+1). Across ten wavelengths and two chords, twenty cases, prediction and measurement differ by at most 0.00000, and a 0.600 mm undulation at lambda=5.00 m gives a versine whose largest absolute value over 200 m is 0.00000 mm. Sorting into third-octave wavelength bands does not remove it (the ratio spans 0.00041 to 2.000, a factor of 4924), and the operator's total_power earned its place here: the band sum is only 0.519 of the sum over all FFT bins, and the missing 48 % is the 30 m irregularity sitting below f_min — invisible if only the bands are read. Dividing by |H| to invert diverges to 6.9e7 mm at the blind wavelengths, and regularising it makes three wavelengths quietly report 'no irregularity'. Two chords (10 m and 6 m) recover eight of the ten wavelengths to within 0.1 %, but the shared blind spot is not a point: it is the comb lambda = 1.000/k. One prediction was wrong — the planted lambda=0.100 m also survived, and it turned out to be the k=10 tooth. The relative spacing of the comb equals lambda itself, so it thickens toward short wavelengths: the corrugation band 0.03-0.30 m alone holds 30 blind wavelengths. At the 0.25 m sampling of a recording car, a 30 mm corrugation reappears as a 0.750 m undulation of 0.0528 mm, nine times the 0.00584 mm floor of a control with the short waves switched off. An asymmetric chord (3.7 m and 6.3 m arms) removes the long-wave blind spots, but |H| = 0 requires a/lambda and b/lambda to be integers together, so the blind spots move to lambda = gcd(a, b)/k = 0.100 m: the chord built to measure corrugation is blind inside the corrugation band. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_rail_corrugation.py)
- [Pièces de monnaie réelles](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_coin_metrology/01_scene.png) (figure): The real photograph famous for 'a global threshold cannot work here, the lighting falls away across the frame' (scikit-image coins). The background does slope, 0.427 to 0.161 down the rows and 0.331 to 0.059 across the columns — and yet a plain global Otsu with hole filling and an area floor of 150 lands exactly on the true 24. The truth is fixed by three independent paths agreeing (an area plateau spanning 50 to 800, a Hough circle search with the radii stated explicitly, and Sobel plus hole filling) and then checked one-to-one, each circle falling inside exactly one component. The margin, however, is 0.05: adding a little more of the same slope drops the count to 22. A correct answer is not evidence of a safe one. At +0.30 the median area moves by only -0.33 % while the worst single coin moves -27.26 %, and the drift correlates with row position at r = -0.91 — true area does not depend on where a coin sits, so that correlation is error, entire. Flattening with gray_tophat keeps the count but shrinks the median area to 0.29x, and the raw component count differs by 30 % between 4- and 8-connectivity. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py)
- [Rotation de textures réelles](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_texture_invariance/01_textures.png) (figure): Three real textures (brick, grass, gravel, all CC0) rotated by known angles, measuring how far the descriptor drifts from itself. The benchmark is set before measuring: the smallest distance between materials, 0.01250 between grass and gravel, is the resolution of the task, and any rotation drift beyond it means a rotated sample is further from itself than from a different material. The anisotropic brick drifts 0.0433 at five degrees and 0.1205 at sixty, 9.6 times the resolution, while grass and gravel stay at 0.0005 to 0.0024, effectively invariant. Two controls isolate the cause: interpolation alone, measured by rotating plus seven and back minus seven degrees, contributes 0.03685 for brick, and an exact ninety-degree rotation with no interpolation at all still gives 0.08501, or 6.8 times the resolution. Anisotropy can be measured independently and explains the ranking: the global concentration of gradient orientation is 0.309 for brick against 0.027 and 0.029 for the others. Switching to rotation-invariant encodings brings brick from 9.64 down through 5.49 to 1.72 — never below one — while the isotropic pair improves tenfold, from 0.17 to 0.02. LBP's rotation invariance applies to the cyclic shift of a local pattern; it cannot remove the orientation distribution of the material itself. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_texture_invariance.py)
- [Défauts des rouleaux de transport](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_web_roll_periodicity/01_scene_web.png) (figure): In roll-to-roll production of film, battery electrode, copper foil and paper, a single scar on a conveying roller prints itself onto the web once per roller circumference. Can the machine-direction spectrum of a defect map recover that circumference and name the culprit against a table of pi*D? Reading the naive spectral maximum names a real but innocent roller: an impulse train has a comb whose harmonics are as tall as the fundamental, so the maximum grabs C/2 = 235.62 mm, and that lands on the cooling roller at 314.16 mm in the table. A circumference that exists nowhere would be caught; one that exists elsewhere in the table passes review. That error stays at 235.9 mm as the miss rate goes from 0 to 50 % — a constant error is not evidence of robustness, it is the same mistake repeated. The fix is fail-closed: take the lowest frequency at which harmonics k=1..3 all stand. The null baseline, the median spacing between detected defects, is right when detection is perfect (-0.03 mm) but at a 40 % miss rate it errs by 472.1 mm and the mean by 813.5 mm, while the comb method holds at 3.2 mm — a missed defect removes amplitude without moving phase. A 25 mm meander, uncorrected, splits one roller's defect line into two across the web (periodic defects remaining in the lane fall from 100 % to 44 %), yet the MD spectrum is bit-for-bit unchanged: meander only breaks methods that cut lanes in the cross direction. Sixty control trials with only healthy rollers give a 1.7 % false-positive rate, and the floor is not zero — the healthy side reaches 3.99 times the band median, above the 2.5 threshold on its own, so what actually holds the line is the demand that every harmonic stand. There are two cliffs. The prediction L_crit = C^2/dC = 14137 mm matches the measured resolution cliff at 14000 mm, a ratio of 0.99. But the detection cliff, the shortest record that still always reports, is 17000 mm — longer. Shortening the record makes you say nothing before it makes you say the wrong roller, so the cliff Rayleigh predicted correctly is never reached. The criterion itself needed repair: at 24 trials a clean 0 % existed, at 120 trials 2 % survives even at the longest record, so 0 % was a small-sample artefact rather than a floor. The most frequent wrong answer, at seven of nine sweep points, is the cooling roller, and 471.24 / 314.16 = 1.500 is exactly 3:2: an integer ratio inside the table gives the comb method a misidentification of its own. The two gaps this PoC found have since been filled: fs.point_spectrum takes a periodogram straight from event positions and carries its own resolution in the return value, and fs.peak_subbin refines a peak below the bin without clamping the shift, since a vertex further than half a sample away means the index was not a maximum at all. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_web_roll_periodicity.py)
- [Balayage du cordon de soudure](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/10_stripe_width.png) (figure): A fillet-weld cross-section is built in closed form with convexity, leg lengths, throat and undercut depth varied along the weld line by known functions, then the triangulation angle is swept from 15 to 70 degrees. Height resolution improves as 1/sin(theta) while any face rising steeper than cot(theta) hides in its own shadow, so the far parent plate turns away exactly at the predicted 37.0 degrees and the far undercut starts vanishing earlier, at 20.8-34.5 degrees (the deeper the groove, the sooner).
